/**
 * Definition for singly-linked list.
 * function ListNode(val, next) {
 *     this.val = (val===undefined ? 0 : val)
 *     this.next = (next===undefined ? null : next)
 * }
 */
/**
 * @param {ListNode} head
 * @return {ListNode}
 */
var swapPairs = function(head) {
    let dummy = new ListNode(0)
    dummy.next = head
    let prev = dummy
    let curr = head
    while ( curr !== null && curr.next !== null ) {
        let _next = curr.next
        curr.next = _next.next
        _next.next = curr
        prev.next = _next
    
        prev = curr
        curr = curr.next
    }
    return dummy.next
}; ^XCB8JExX

Brute Force Approach ^UR2pogWD

var swapPairs = function(head) {
    if (!head) return null
    let listElements = []
    let curr = head
    while ( curr !== null ) {
        listElements.push(curr.val)
        curr = curr.next
    }
    // swap the adjacent ones
    for ( let i = 0;  i < listElements.length;) {
        if (i+1 >= listElements.length ) {
            break
        }
        [listElements[i+1], listElements[i]]= [listElements[i], listElements[i+1]]
        i += 2
    }
    let newHead = new ListNode(listElements[0])
    let currNew = newHead
    for ( let i = 0 ; i < listElements.length; i++ ) {
        const next = listElements[i+1]
        if ( i+1 >= listElements.length ) {
            break
        }
        currNew.next = new ListNode(listElements[i+1])
        currNew = currNew.next
    }
    return newHead
} ^NPOoMFIB
